<span class="confinace__blockquote--star"><i aria-hidden="true" class="fa fa-star"></i><i aria-hidden="true" class="fa fa-star"></i><i aria-hidden="true" class="fa fa-star"></i><i aria-hidden="true" class="fa fa-star"></i><i aria-hidden="true" class="fa fa-star"></i></span>

</blockquote>
</div>
<div class="Tcolumn-xl-4 Tcolumn-lg-6 Tcolumn-sm-12 confiance__item">

Mais entre temps vous avez eu ce que je voulais en stock et pour moins cher. Encore merci et je continuerai sans doute à commander chez vous. »</p>

    var e = [],
        t = new Date;

        var e = this.getMonth() + 1,
            t = this.getDate();

    };

                s < 864e5 && s > 1e3 && e.push(s)
            }

                if (e) {
                    String(e).length < 6 && String(e).indexOf(":") > -1 && (e = a + " " + String(e)), -1 == String(e).indexOf(":") && (e += " 00:00");
                    var i = e.split(":"),
                        o = String(i.slice(0, 1)),
                        n = o.substr(o.length - 2),
                        l = i.slice(1),
                        r = n < 24 && l < 60;
                    return "Invalid Date" != new Date(e) && r || $(s).addClass("error").attr("title", '"' + e + '" date is incorrect; please use YYYY-MM-DD HH:MM format'), new Date(e).getTime()
                }

            }

    }
}

function CountDownWidget(e) {
    this.$el = e, this.countDownEnd = new Date(e.data("end")), this.$elDays = e.find(".count-down-days").show(), this.$elHours = e.find(".count-down-hours").show(), this.$elMinutes = e.find(".count-down-minutes").show(), this.$elSeconds = e.find(".count-down-seconds").show(), this.$elMilliseconds = e.find(".count-down-milliseconds").show(), this.$elFinish = e.find(".count-down-finish").hide(), this.tick = function() {
        var e = 100,
            t = Math.floor((this.countDownEnd - new Date) / 10);
        if (t < 0) return this.$elFinish.show(), this.$elDays.hide(), this.$elHours.hide(), this.$elMinutes.hide(), this.$elSeconds.hide(), void this.$elMilliseconds.hide();
        var a = Math.floor(t / 864e4),
            s = Math.floor(t % 864e4 / 36e4),
            i = Math.floor(t % 36e4 / 6e3),
            o = Math.floor(t % 6e3 / e),
            n = Math.floor(t % e);
        this.$elDays.length ? this.$elDays.text(("00" + a).slice(-2)) : s += 24 * a, this.$elHours.text(("00" + s).slice(-2)), this.$elMinutes.text(("00" + i).slice(-2)), this.$elSeconds.text(("00" + o).slice(-2)), this.$elMilliseconds.text(("00" + n).slice(-2))
    }, this.start = function() {
        this._interval = setInterval(this.tick.bind(this), 220)
    }, this.stop = function() {
        clearInterval(this._interval)
    }
}
